This choice has zero new paid API use and preserves approval boundary. The SHA chain can detect simple local corruption, not independent notarization; a malicious DB administrator can rewrite an entire chain.
6. **Security/limits.** SQLite connection and logical time are supplied by caller; G119 CLI explicitly creates and deletes a disposable local SQLite file, uses no owner paths. The event log stores stable template IDs and role/result statuses, not data content. Fresh, isolated runtime/external anchoring/cross-process crash recovery are not complete. Proactive timestamp is caller supplied (not trusted UTC); state not persisted across CLI invocations. Proposals are not notifications, no NAS watcher, no automatic writes/backup and no autonomous LLM self-healing. G116 repair is a single fixed missing-edge template repair, NOT arbitrary code patching.

## G88d — 2026-10-31 03:40 KST · Windows PowerShell runner correction

1. **Goal/stack:** Verify G88 review functionality on owner's existing Windows PowerShell checkout using Python stdlib unittest, without NAS.
2. **Confirmed owner evidence:** Their HAE checkout ran 14 tests PASS/0 FAIL with exit 0; this is **HAE only**, not a UGREEN test. User correctly changed to `_g84_local/UGREEN`, pulled `lead/ugreen` to exact SHA `c0386f464395f5fea8ef6db7599a1f2be5dffd34`, then `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File .\\verify_local.ps1 -Mode g88` reported `UGREEN LOCAL VERIFY: FAIL [g88]` with **runner exit code 2**. Owner-local log path is system Temp `ugreen-flowpilot-verify/verify-20261031-033901-19648.log` and summary matching `.json`. Their actual log text was **not provided**, so test-case failures and root cause are **unconfirmed**.
3. **Root cause hypothesis and minimal fix:** The previous `verify_local.ps1` used global `$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"` while piping Python `unittest -v 2>&1` through `Tee-Object`. Windows PowerShell 5.1 can classify normal unittest stderr output as a terminating `NativeCommandError`, yielding wrapper catch exit 2 rather than the real Python test exit code. G88d scopes `$ErrorActionPreference = "Continue"` only to the native unittest pipeline, captures `$LASTEXITCODE`, restores strict error handling in `finally`, and prints 20 lines of local failure log when exit nonzero. Does not suppress actual Python exit-code failures.
4. **Validation/limitations:** GitHub code commit `8f8f5215447f08b2c52905576ac1930fbb1a4c20`, `hibi89/hibi89-ugreen-nas-hackathon@lead/ugreen`, remote source readback PASS for the modified runner. **PowerShell runtime test of G88d and Python G88 functional suite NOT YET EXECUTED/OBSERVED; fix is unverified.** Prior G87 Actions 826 PASS/0 FAIL/5 SKIP is different SHA; do not use to promote G88. Real Uliya Expert/NAS NOT_RUN, release HOLD.
5. **Stop/retry:** Initial wrong-directory issue fixed by owner, do not repeat. One runner failure at exact SHA recorded; remediate root hypothesis then retry only once. If repeat fails, inspect actual new log/summary rather than blind patching. Do not infer all 10 G88 tests were executed by the 03:39 failure.

## G82 — 2026-10-31 KST · backend first: HAE/LA at-most-once write recovery + preview identity binding

1. **Goal / confirmed owner requirements / tech:** STOP PDF, visual polishing and Devpost until core backend is working. Repair real risk at G81 `FlowPilot → HAE localhost → Lightning Azent → SQLite` when a write might have committed but HTTP 500/422, malformed JSON, redirect or transport drop makes the result unknown.
